# Streamline one repository

This proposal makes three planes smaller and clearer:

- Plane A makes examples into curated suites and honest checks.
- Plane B makes docs keep one home for each kind of truth.
- Plane C makes the repository root and session output bounded.

The example map covers 681 current .jet files. It assigns 488 files to 52
target suites, keeps 185 files in their current special roles, and deletes
8 duplicate or probe files. The target inventory is 237 entries: 52 suite
entries plus 185 kept entries. The eight deleted entries do not return.

The docs census covers 131 files and 133,567 lines. It keeps 64 files and
removes 67 sources from the active tree. The 67 consist of 37 direct deletes
and 30 fold-then-delete sources.

Plane C adds 18 exact ignore patterns. They block root probes, session logs,
parity reports, parked files, partial binaries, and the tracked run cache.
Commits 6675d936 and c86657b2e already purged these junk classes once. The
policy below prevents their return.

No implementation is part of this proposal write. Four owner ballots decide
the changes. The ballots amend D-REPO-EXAMPLES1 where stated and preserve
D-EXAMPLES-SHORTPATH1.

## Owner gates

| Gate | Result |
|---|---|
| New user syntax | None. Use current syntax only: - arrows, split effect rows such as -[IO]>, failure suffixes such as Entry? StoreError!, defaults such as timeout: Int{30}, and loop in. |
| New standard-library dependency | None. |
| I9 tier exception | None. Every applicable suite keeps one meaning across AOT, default Cranelift jet run, interpreter, and web paths. |
| Owner decisions | D-EXAMPLES-SUITES1, D-DOCS-GC1, D-REPO-LAYOUT1, and D-DOCS-SSOT1. |

## Plane A — Examples

### Target shape

Use two example layers:

~~~text
examples/
  suites/
    curated runnable stories
    expected/
  checks/
    compile-fail, diagnostic, target, host, harness, and gate proofs
    expected/
  learn/
  interop/
  jetpack/
  performance/
  continuity/
  canon.jet
~~~

The current features tree becomes the checks layer during migration. Positive
teaching stories move to suites. Negative, target-only, host-provisioned,
package, FFI, service, and harness cases stay checks or move to checks.

Apply these rules:

1. Give each suite one user story, one default entry, and one deterministic
   output contract.
2. Compose mechanisms only when they share data, effects, inputs, cleanup,
   and output.
3. Put the safe magic-first path in the unsuffixed suite.
4. Add an _expert sibling only for a real manual tradeoff.
5. Keep one executable oracle across all applicable execution tiers.
6. Fix clocks, seeds, ports, input bytes, and environment data.
7. Explain mechanisms in suite comments. Put bare closure proof in checks.
8. Use current ratified syntax. Do not copy retired spellings.

The rules come from suite-design.md, examples/README.md, and the example
philosophy. The flagship rule is D-EXAMPLES-SHORTPATH1=A. The expert path is
visible beside the beginner path, never in its place.

current merge member exists. Do not invent code only to fill that target name.

### Auxiliary trees and fold rules

| Current area | Treatment |
|---|---|
| examples/canon.jet | Stay. It is the compiling syntax and release-gate reference. Link suites for stories. |
| examples/learn/ | Stay. Keep every broken and solved teaching pair. Link its solved path to the matching suite after the first arc. |
| examples/interop/ | Stay. Keep one external runtime or ABI per project directory. Do not merge toolchains into a suite. |
| examples/jetpack/ | Stay. Keep environment, package, workspace, image, and Hangar recovery examples in the project and configuration lane. |
| examples/performance/ | Stay. Keep artifact budgets and service readiness as receipt fixtures, not ordinary output goldens. |
| examples/continuity/ | Stay. Keep the package-to-service-to-executable journey. Link it from packages and devloop. |
| examples/features/ positive stories | Move to examples/suites/. Use the mapped target and delete replaced duplicate sources after proof. |
| examples/features/ negative, target, host, package, FFI, service, and harness cases | Move to examples/checks/. Keep their special expected channels and gate identity. |

The flagship and expert rule applies inside suites. The unsuffixed file is the
first README link. An expert sibling stays adjacent when it teaches raw
memory, manual task wiring, process policy, browser protocol, or target
control. First-hour and UI paths retain their existing expert pairing while
the target suite names stay domain-first.

### Golden migration

Expected output is a path mirror. Move each source tree with every owned
channel:

~~~text
examples/features/<old-stem>.jet
examples/features/expected/<old-stem>.*
        becomes
examples/suites/<new-stem>.jet
examples/suites/expected/<new-stem>.*
~~~

Checks use the same mirror under examples/checks/. Do not change suffix
meanings:

- .out is plain successful jet run stdout.
- .err.out is an expected non-zero result.
- .stderr.out is incidental stderr from a successful result.
- .web.out is web or Wasm stdout.
- .harness.out is DOM interaction output.
- .seed.out and .greet.out are named job outputs.
- .test.out is the jet test report.
- .fuzz.out is the jet fuzz report.

Keep one plain .out per suite. A special channel remains special. A job keeps
its job-name suffix. A package keeps package and module roots. A web case keeps
its HTML and harness output. Error output with source positions gets a manual
review after the move.

The merge needs a stable proof map. It must contain old path, new suite, new
case identity, package or root kind, expected channels, harnesses, docs, and
literal consumers. Do not create a second path fallback. Extend current
collectors only enough to discover the new suite and check paths. This is path
migration, not a general harness rewrite.

| Old family | New home | Required check |
|---|---|---|
| compute_autodiff, compute_cuda, compute_device, compute_kernel, compute_linalg, compute_metal, compute_ml, compute_ndarray, compute_simd, compute_tensor, compute_vulkan_webgpu | math.jet | Dedicated compute or backend capability checks. |
| compute_views | collections_views_expert.jet | Tensor-view check. |
| data_bridges | tooling_data.jet | Bridge capability check. |
| solve_puzzle | math.jet | Solver check. |
| dns_lookup, email_dkim, email_message | net_http.jet | Specialized DNS and email checks. |
| concurrency/protocol | concurrency.jet | Generated-protocol check. |
| crypto_migration, random_api_split | crypto.jet | Migration and randomness-policy checks. |
| serde/encoding_base_expert/run.jet | serde_streams_expert.jet | Keep expert codec pairing and a codec check. |
| comptime/doctests.jet | tooling_tests.jet | Doctest check. |
| collections/sketch, collections/zip_family | collections.jet | Approximate-structure and collection/archive checks. |

The five riskiest path changes are:

- tooling/service_runtime.jet to tooling_services.jet. Consumers include
  tests/services_runtime.rs:18-66 and tests/fixtures/examples_panic_budget.txt:63.
- concurrency/parallel_scan.jet to concurrency.jet. Preserve the manual
  channel, spawn, and join behavior in the expert sibling.
- serde/encoding_json_stream.jet to serde_streams_expert.jet. Consumers include
  tests/encoding_parity.rs:1455-1468 and tests/jit_corpus_gate.txt:53.
- web/ui_web_click.jet to web_wasm.jet. Consumers include
  tests/fuzz/sema/differential/ex_web_ui_web_click.jet:15,21,
  tests/web_browser.rs:169, and tests/web_build.rs:3409.
- io/cli.jet to cli.jet. Consumers include
  tests/fuzz/sema/differential/ex_concurrency_parallel_scan.jet:7 and
  tests/release_gates.rs:217.

### Ordered migration recipe

The following recipe comes from golden-machinery.md. Run it only in a
write-enabled implementation lane.

1. Capture the old path set.

~~~sh
git status --short

JET_MIGRATION_SCRATCH="$HOME/.cache/jet-test-scratch/golden-machinery-merge"
mkdir -p "$JET_MIGRATION_SCRATCH"

find examples/features -type f -name '*.jet' -not -path '*/.jet/*' -print \
  | sort > "$JET_MIGRATION_SCRATCH/feature-paths.before"

rg -n "examples/" tests/ Source/ crates/ tools/ scripts/ .github/ docs/ build.rs README.md AGENTS.md flake.nix llms.text \
  > "$JET_MIGRATION_SCRATCH/examples-refs.before"
~~~

2. Define the new proof identity before moving files. Complete every column
   in the old-to-new map. Preserve logical cases inside a suite.

3. Classify each old path as ordinary golden, special golden, web, nested
   run.jet, package, job or test, FFI or polyglot, safety or diagnostic, or
   non-feature corpus.

4. Move source trees and expected channels as units. Move package roots,
   foreign files, HTML, job outputs, and source-map text together.

5. Update discovery and case identity in tests/golden.rs:177-225,
   tests/dev_parts/support.rs:991-1057, tools/ci/compose-corpus-gate.sh:90-108,
   tests/truthfulness.rs:484-598, and tests/fuzz_sema.rs:53-105.

6. Update literal consumers. Include stdin tables, service predicates,
   safety rows, panic budgets, feature claims, proof targets, web docs, CLI
   help, learn flows, release gates, CI, WASI scripts, and performance rows.

7. Regenerate the corpus gate with the exact command:

~~~sh
JET_CORPUS_GATE_REPORT_DIR=jit-aot-parity-report JET_WRITE_CORPUS_GATE=1 \
  bash tools/ci/jit-aot-parity.sh
~~~

Review missing, ghost, duplicate, held-out, and reason changes. Do not edit
tests/jit_corpus_gate.txt by hand.

8. Re-bless one ordinary case at a time after review:

~~~sh
scripts/agent/jet-env env \
  JET_GOLDEN_FILTER=examples/features/<new-stem>.jet \
  JET_UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 \
  cargo test --test golden examples_compile_and_run -- --nocapture
~~~

During the bridge, use the exact feature identity above. After the collector
uses the final tree, use the equivalent examples/suites or examples/checks
identity without changing the filter contract.

For a target that owns UPDATE_EXPECT, use only its own target:

~~~sh
scripts/agent/jet-env env \
  UPDATE_EXPECT=1 \
  UPDATE_EXPECT_REASON="examples suite merge: <reviewed reason>" \
  cargo test --test <owning-target>
~~~

9. Hand-update ratchets and generated metadata from the reviewed map:
   tests/fixtures/examples_panic_budget.txt, docs/spec/safety.md,
   tests/retirement_ratchet.rs, docs/spec/feature-claims.json,
   docs/spec/proof-replay-decisions.md, tools/perf/corpus.tsv,
   tools/perf/baseline.json, and llms.text. Generate the last file with:

~~~sh
scripts/agent/jet-env jet inspect digest > llms.text
~~~

10. Check stale paths:

~~~sh
rg -n "examples/" tests/ Source/ crates/ tools/ scripts/ .github/ docs/ build.rs README.md AGENTS.md flake.nix llms.text
scripts/agent/jet-env jet self devtools check-fixture-paths
git diff --check
~~~

Review every remaining old path. Keep it only for a stable corpus,
historical record, synthetic fixture, unchanged canonical path, or explicit
special case.

11. Run the composed proof set:

~~~sh
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test golden examples_compile_and_run -- --nocapture
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test truthfulness
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test ban_bare_panic
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test dev_corpus_gate
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test fuzz_sema
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test web_examples_doc
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test web_build
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test web_browser
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test web_dev
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test web_partition
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test release_gates
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test example_artifacts
~~~

Then run affected literal targets, performance checks, and target-specific
CI or WASI checks.

### Atomic versus incremental work

| Change | Partial state | Atomic boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Add new suites while all old sources stay complete | Safe if new proof also exists. | New source, channels, docs, and test entries land together. |
| Rename or delete a direct golden source | Unsafe. | Source, every suffix, corpus row, docs path, literals, and receipt identity change together. |
| Merge cases into one suite | Unsafe without preserved case identity. | Suite source, case map, expected outputs, parity, fuzz, stdin, service, and docs agree. |
| Change the corpus manifest | Unsafe. | Live source set, shards, manifest, held-out classes, and floors are generated and reviewed together. |
| Change panic or safety rows | Unsafe for affected sources. | Source content, sorted rows, counts, reasons, line ranges, and gated inputs change together. |
| Move package, web, FFI, or interop trees | Unsafe. | All source, manifests, modules, foreign files, HTML, harnesses, and expected files move together. |
| Update active docs after source moves | Safe only while old source exists. | All truthfulness-scanned docs point to live paths before deletion. |
| Change performance identity or build source paths | Unsafe. | Sources, expected outputs, hashes, baselines, build metadata, CI, and rerun behavior agree. |

The safe intermediate state is old proof complete plus new proof additive. The
requested final state is atomic across the map and every listed consumer.

## Plane B — Docs

### Canonical layout

~~~text
docs/
  README.md, first-hour.md, diagnostic-recovery.md
    User navigation and first-use recovery.
  spec/
    Binding, architecture, diagnostic, API, release, and policy law.
  reference/
    User-facing API and command reference.
  plans/
    Durable epoch plans and current implementation sequencing.
  proposals/
    README plus unexplored owner choices.
  research/
    Active research with a live card, owner gate, defect, constraint, or consumer.
  audits/
    Current evidence for an open gate, consumer, or contradiction.
  sidequests/
    Exceptional cross-epoch plans until specs, examples, and tests absorb them.
  ballots/
    Hidden Tower durability payloads only.
  archive/
    Historical research, closed audits, and superseded proposals.
  agents/
    Live agent operating infrastructure.
  adr/
    Future durable decisions without a better existing spec home.
~~~

The active tree keeps law in spec, user guidance in reference, and live
planning in plans. Finished evidence leaves the active list. Archive remains
hidden from the active navigation. The existing accepted cleanup plan remains
the source for this shape.

## Plane C — Repository layout

### Root layout law

The following list is exhaustive for intentional root entries. Every other
root entry is forbidden except for the scoped ephemeral entries below.

| Root entry | Purpose |
|---|---|
| .agents/ | Canonical agent roles, skills, shared lenses, and marketplace metadata. |
| .claude/ | Claude integration, settings, and compatibility symlinks only. |
| .cursor/ | Cursor rules and links to vendored Tower skills. |
| .envrc | nix-direnv entry point and shell selection. |
| .github/ | GitHub CI and release workflows. |
| .gitignore | Build, cache, generated, and session-output boundary. |
| .jet/ | Managed project state, lock, proofs, performance records, and bounded child state. |
| .zed/ | Root Zed settings for .jet files and jet-lsp. |
| AGENTS.md | Canonical agent operating manual. |
| CLAUDE.md | Compatibility symlink to AGENTS.md. |
| Cargo.lock | Rust workspace dependency lock. |
| Cargo.toml | Rust workspace and package entry points. |
| LICENSE | MIT license notice. |
| README.md | Project introduction, commands, and repository map. |
| Source/ | Jet host, compiler commands, executors, interpreter, LSP, and adapters. |
| assets/ | Product media. |
| build.rs | Build script and compiler source fingerprint inputs. |
| clippy.toml | Static safety restrictions. |
| corelib/ | First-party Core packages and timezone data. |
| crates/ | Rust compiler seams, runtime, JIT, CLI, package manager, and product crates. |
| docs/ | Specs, references, research, audits, proposals, plans, and continuity. |
| editors/ | Committed editor and tree-sitter sources. |
| env.jet | Root Jet-native environment projection. |
| examples/ | Executable feature specifications and supporting corpora. |
| flake.lock | Pinned Nix input revisions. |
| flake.nix | Nix build and development shells. |
| llms.text | Generated LLM language-surface digest. |
| plugins/ | Vendored project plugins and Tower. |
| scripts/ | Agent guards, orchestration, harnesses, and development scripts. |
| skills-lock.json | External agent-skill source and content-hash lock. |
| tests/ | Rust integration tests, snapshots, parity tests, fixtures, and workloads. |
| tools/ | CI gates, Nix tools, performance dashboard, and corpus utilities. |

Ephemeral root exceptions are .git/, target/, target-<name>/, build/, .tmp/,
and .claude/worktrees/<short-name> or .agent-worktrees/<short-name>. They are
never tracked as project source. target/ is the normal checkout cache.
target-<name>/ exists only for one active isolated session. build/ is compiler
output. .tmp/ is command-owned scratch. Worktrees are integrated and removed
promptly, with .claude/worktrees preferred.

Numbered reports, probes, parity outputs, and lane logs belong under
$HOME/.cache/jet-luna/<lane>/<run-id>/. The streamline output directory is the
one required external exception. Test scratch belongs under
$HOME/.cache/jet-test-scratch or a command-owned .tmp/<lane>/<run-id>/.
Delete each run directory at lane close. Keep only mandated final reports.
Never put session output at the repository root or in tracked .claude/.

### Exact .gitignore additions

Add these exact patterns:

~~~gitignore
/.checked_text_probe.jet
/card2054_nested.jet
/debug_drain.jet
/.corpus-gate-test.log
/card*-cargo-noop.sh
/.agent-scratch-*/
/jit-aot-parity-report-*/
/.canvas-cargo-bin/
/.claude/*.log
/.claude/bdlog/*.log
/.claude/parked/
/.claude/probe/
/.claude/binpat_prelude_backup/
/.claude/*.txt
/.claude/*.diff
/.claude/*.patch
**/.jet/bin/*.partial
/.cache/
~~~

The root .jet entries are exact. Do not use a broad /*.jet rule because env.jet
is load-bearing. Add /src/ only after the owner confirms Source/ is the
permanent Rust root. Ignore rules do not remove tracked paths. Untrack or
delete existing .claude logs, .cache/jet/run artifacts, and root src/ in their
own accepted cleanup change.

| Area | Evidence | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| .agents/ and .claude/skills/ | .claude/skills/ has 42 links into ../../.agents/skills. Its real extra is burndown/SKILL.md. | Keep .agents/ canonical. Merge the extra instructions once, then delete the real .claude copy. |
| .cursor/skills/ and .agents/skills/ | Six Cursor links target plugins/tower/skills. | Keep as a thin Cursor adapter. |

| target/ and target-nixfeed/ | target/ is about 111 GiB. target-nixfeed/ is 591 MiB. | Keep target/. Keep target-nixfeed/ only for an active named session. |
| .agent-worktrees/ and .claude/worktrees/ | Both are empty. AGENTS.md prefers .claude/worktrees/. | Keep the preferred root. Retire the alternate after migration. |
| .opencode/ | Empty, untracked, and has no in-repository consumer. | Delete if no external session owns it. |
| env.jet and flake.nix | They are related but distinct environment projections. | Keep both at root. Add a drift check only if facts need enforcement. |
| skills-lock.json and .agents/skills/ | The lock records external paths and hashes. No in-repo reader owns its location. | Keep the lock at root until the external consumer confirms a move. |

Evidence includes .claude/skills/, .agents/skills/, plugins/tower/README.md:26-34,
docs/agents/orchestration.md:101-110,164-170, editors/zed/install.sh:23-50,
Source/RunCache.rs:75-90, scripts/agent/jet-env:56-75, and the cited
repo-hygiene.md records.

### .jet/ growth bound

.jet/lock is durable semantic state. The committed object is 2,147,495,440
bytes. Its provenance line is 2,147,484,058 bytes. The observed growth is
536,882,439 bytes at a06b4ffc1, 1,073,753,532 bytes at 4b5246b96, and
2,147,495,440 bytes at 9cac4d015.

The cause is repeated escaping. NixIdentity creates quoted JSON, local-Nix
realization prefixes it, the writer escapes it, and the envelope parser trims
quotes without unescaping. The next write doubles the representation.

Set one finite input and output bound for .jet/lock. Reject oversized input
before parsing and oversized output before publication. Choose the exact byte
constant from a sane lock corpus in the repair card. Do not hand-edit the
current durable lock.

Parse all quoted scalar fields through one unescape path. Add a round-trip test
with quoted and backslashed provenance. Route every lock rewrite through the
shared atomic writer. Keep .jet/build, .jet/receipts, generated bindings, and
local memory or fuzz output disposable and ignored. Keep .jet/proofs,
.jet/perf/*.jettrace, and .jet/replays only as bounded durable evidence.

The live 11,920-byte lock edit is concurrent state. Preserve it for separate
review. It does not prove the historical 2.0 GiB writer is fixed.

### jet-timing.json

Timed output must write only to an explicit caller-owned report or scratch
directory. It must never write a second copy to the process cwd.

Make JET_TIMING_DIR the single output contract. Give LSP an explicit directory
or an external cache default. Update the timing spec and tests together. Keep
the current ignore entries as a safety net, not as ownership.

The current recurrence comes from PhaseTiming::write_to writing both the
requested directory and cwd. The interpreter passes cwd, LSP appends to cwd,
and BudgetProviders sets JET_TIMING_DIR to the project root. Evidence:
crates/jet-driver/src/PhaseTiming.rs:19-22,88-105,
Source/Interpreter.rs:1025-1027, Source/LSP/Server.rs:431-439,
tests/build_entry.rs:379-403, tools/perf/dashboard.sh:492-514,526-540,
Source/BudgetProviders.rs:1917-1928, and docs/spec/spec.md:1284-1298.

## Non-goals

- No syntax changes.
- No harness redesign beyond path migration.
- No compatibility shims, aliases, fallback parsers, or legacy readers.
- No language, compiler, runtime, stdlib, ABI, or output behavior changes.
- No movement of learn, interop, Jetpack, performance, continuity, or canon
  into ordinary suites.
- No Tower mutation in this proposal lane.

## Rollout

Each phase is one coherent change. Do not merge a phase with a known
contradictory proof or an unrecorded owner gate.

### Phase 0 — Ratify the four choices and freeze identity

Record the four ballot decisions. Freeze the 681-row mapping and the docs
delete, fold, and hold lists. Add no new source path.

Proof:

~~~sh
jq empty /home/nate/.cache/jet-luna/streamline/ballots/D-EXAMPLES-SUITES1.json
jq empty /home/nate/.cache/jet-luna/streamline/ballots/D-DOCS-GC1.json
jq empty /home/nate/.cache/jet-luna/streamline/ballots/D-REPO-LAYOUT1.json
jq empty /home/nate/.cache/jet-luna/streamline/ballots/D-DOCS-SSOT1.json
~~~

### Phase 1 — Add suite and check discovery

Add the target directories, path-aware discovery, expected mirrors, and the
old-to-new proof identity map. Keep every old source and expected channel.

Proof:

~~~sh
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test golden examples_compile_and_run -- --nocapture
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test truthfulness
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test example_artifacts
git diff --check
~~~

### Phase 2 — Migrate one suite cluster

Move one language, safety, or application cluster as an additive change.
Preserve source stories, expert siblings, special channels, and the map.

Proof:

~~~sh
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test golden examples_compile_and_run -- --nocapture
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test ban_bare_panic
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test dev_corpus_gate
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test run_interpret
~~~

### Phase 3 — Migrate special tiers and checks

Move package, web, FFI, target, job, test, fuzz, service, and performance
cases. Resolve every listed coverage hole. Delete no old source until its
replacement proof is green.

Proof:

~~~sh
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test fuzz_sema
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test web_examples_doc
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test web_build
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test web_browser
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test web_dev
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test web_partition
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test release_gates
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test example_artifacts
~~~

### Phase 4 — Close the example merge

Delete replaced positive sources and eight duplicate or probe files. Update
all consumers, expected paths, corpus rows, ratchets, claims, baselines,
README links, and llms.text. Run the full composed proof set from Plane A.

Proof:

~~~sh
rg -n "examples/" tests/ Source/ crates/ tools/ scripts/ .github/ docs/ build.rs README.md AGENTS.md flake.nix llms.text
scripts/agent/jet-env jet self devtools check-fixture-paths
git diff --check
~~~

Then run the eleven commands in the composed proof block.

### Phase 5 — Apply docs GC and SSOT

Fold missing law, update hard references, correct drift findings F1 through F11,
delete the 67 listed sources, and keep the 64 hold files until their questions
have owner records.

Proof:

~~~sh
rg -n "examples/features|pkg\\.jet|jet store gc|Type\\.|:>|:\\[" docs README.md examples/README.md llms.text
git diff --check
scripts/agent/jet-env cargo test --test truthfulness
~~~

Review intentional historical and diagnostic matches before deletion.

### Phase 6 — Enforce root and session boundaries

Add the exact ignore patterns. Move new reports and probes to lane cache.
Remove or untrack vestigial output only in its accepted cleanup change. Repair
lock parsing, size checks, atomic publication, and timing cwd writes in their
own implementation patch.

Proof:

~~~sh
git check-ignore -v .checked_text_probe.jet .cache/jet/run/module.bin
git diff --check
scripts/agent/disk-report.sh
~~~

Do not claim the lock or timing defects fixed from this proposal alone.

## Verification

An implementer proves each phase with the commands named in that phase and
keeps the old-to-new map beside the patch. The final example proof must cover
parser, sema, TIR, AOT, default Cranelift jet run, interpreter, and web where
the feature applies. The final docs proof must show no active stale path and
no lost hard consumer. The final layout proof must show no new root junk,
bounded session output, and no cwd timing report.

Owner visual acceptance is limited to:

- README first links put the safe flagship path before its expert sibling.
- The docs landing page makes the canonical homes and archive boundary clear.
- The suite names and scopes read as domain stories, not mechanism piles.

No owner visual check replaces machine proof.
